\displaystyle\delta t\left(\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x){\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P^{\,x}_{a}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y){\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q^{\,y}_{b}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}.

∎

Appendix B
Deferred Proofs of Section
2.5

Proof of
Lemma

2.32
.

We assume
\left\{A^{x}_{a}\right\}
is projective. Then

\displaystyle\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes B^{x}_{a}\left|\psi\right\rangle\geq\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(B^{x}_{a})^{2}\left|\psi\right\rangle\geq 0,

which implies that

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x}_{a}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes B^{x}_{a}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert\leq\lvert\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x}_{a}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(B^{x}_{a})^{2}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert.

We can bound the second quantity in two steps.

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x}_{a}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x}_{a}\otimes B^{x}_{a}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\lVert A^{x}_{a}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\sqrt{\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\lVert(A^{x}_{a}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes B^{x}_{a})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\delta},

and similarly

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x}\sum_{a}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x}_{a}\otimes B^{x}_{a}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(B^{x}_{a})^{2}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert\leq\sqrt{\delta}.

By the triangle inequality, the second quantity is at most
2\sqrt{\delta}
. So is the first one.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

2.36
.

We start with

\displaystyle A^{x}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{k}}=A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}A^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{k}}.

Because
A^{x}_{a_{k}}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{\delta}\mathds{1}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}
,
To apply
Lemma

2.34
,
we can set
C^{x}_{a,b}=A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}A^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{k-1}}\otimes\mathds{1}

with
a=a_{k}
and
b=(a_{1},\ldots,a_{k-1})
.
Then
\sum_{b}(C^{x}_{a,b})^{\dagger}C^{x}_{a,b}\leq\mathds{1}
.
Hence by
Lemma

2.34

\displaystyle A^{x}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{k}}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{\delta}A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}A^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{k-1}}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}

We can apply
Lemma

2.34
again with
C^{x}_{a,b}=A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}A^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{k-2}}\otimes B_{k}^{(a_{k})}
with
a=a_{k-1}
and
b=(a_{1},\ldots,a_{k-2},a_{k})
.
Because
A^{x}_{a_{k-1}}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{\delta}\mathds{1}\otimes(B_{k-1})^{(a_{k-1})}
, we can get that

\displaystyle A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}A^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{k-1}}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}\approx_{\delta}A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}A^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{k-2}}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}(B_{k-1})^{x}_{a_{k-1}}.

Continuing similarly, we can get that

\displaystyle A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}A^{x}_{a_{1}}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}\approx_{\delta}A^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots A^{x}_{a_{2}}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots(B_{1})^{x}_{a_{1}}.

With another
(k-2)
steps we can get that

\displaystyle A^{x}_{a_{k}}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}(B_{1})^{x}_{a_{1}}(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdot(B_{k-1})^{x}_{a_{k-1}}\approx_{\delta}\mathds{1}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}(B_{1})^{x}_{a_{1}}(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdot(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}.

Combining all the steps above with
Lemma

2.35

\displaystyle A^{x}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{k}}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{(2k-1)^{2}\delta}\mathds{1}\otimes(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}}\cdots(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}(B_{1})^{x}_{a_{1}}(B_{2})^{x}_{a_{2}}\cdot(B_{k})^{x}_{a_{k}},

which completes the proof.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

2.37
, the original proof.

We first show the
k=2
case. Notice that

\displaystyle J^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{1},a_{2}]}=\sum_{g_{2}:g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left(\sum_{g_{1}:g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}}(G_{1})^{x}_{g_{1}}\right)(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}.

Our goal is to bound

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes J^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{1},a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes\sum_{g_{2}:g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle.

First notice that

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\approx_{2\sqrt{2\delta}}\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle=1.

This is because

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\lVert A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\quad\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]})A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]})\left|\psi\right\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\lVert A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1}}\left\langle\psi\right|(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]})\sum_{g_{2}}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]})\left|\psi\right\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq 1\cdot\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1}}\lVert(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\delta}

and

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\cdot(\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\lVert A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1})A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}(\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1})\left|\psi\right\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\lVert A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1})\sum_{a_{1}}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}(\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1})\left|\psi\right\rangle}

\displaystyle\leq 1\cdot\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}}\lVert(\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\delta},

Hence, we focus on proving

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes\left((G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}-(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\delta}+C_{2}\varepsilon

(35)

for some constants
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
, which will imply that

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left((G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}-(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\lVert A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},g_{2}(y_{2})}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\sqrt{\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\lVert\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes\left((G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}-(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{C_{1}\sqrt{\delta}+C_{2}\varepsilon}

and

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes J^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{1},a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle-1\rvert\leq 2\sqrt{2\delta}+\sqrt{C_{1}\sqrt{\delta}+C_{2}\varepsilon}.

To prove
Eq.

35
, we start with
Eq.

9

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{i}}\lVert(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{i})^{x}_{[g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{i}]})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 2\delta

for
i=1,2
.
Then by
Lemma

2.34

\displaystyle\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{2\delta}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{2\delta}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle=A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1}}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{2\delta}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{2}}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{2\delta}\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle.

Chaining the inequalities together using
Lemma

2.35
gives

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes\left((G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 32\delta.

Let

\displaystyle S_{1}

\displaystyle=\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes\left((G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}-(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle S_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes\left((G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}-(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}.

We are going to show that
S_{1}
is close to
S_{2}
.
Expanding
S_{1}-S_{2}
, we get
\lvert S_{1}-S_{2}\rvert\leq\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2}+\Delta_{3}+\Delta_{4}
,
where

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\Delta_{3}

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\Delta_{4}

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert.

First of all

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}=\lvert 1-\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert.

By
Eq.

10
,

\displaystyle\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\approx_{18\delta}A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle,

then
Lemma

2.32
implies that

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle-\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert\leq 6\sqrt{2\delta}.

Since
\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle=1
,

\Delta_{1}\leq 6\sqrt{2\delta}
.
Next, observe that
\Delta_{2}=0
as
(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}
and
(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}
are projective measurements.
Lastly, observe that
\Delta_{3}=\Delta_{4}
, so we focus on bounding
\Delta_{3}
.
First notice that

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{3\sqrt{2\delta}}\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{3\sqrt{2\delta}}\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\otimes(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

The reason why
\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\approx_{18\delta}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\otimes\mathds{1}
is the following.
Applying
Lemma

2.31
to
Eqs.

9
and
10
we get

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{i}}\lVert(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{i})^{x}_{[g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{i}]})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 2\delta

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{i}}\lVert((G_{i})^{x}_{[g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{i}]}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 2\delta.

Notice that for any
i\in[2]
,

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{i}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\geq\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left|\psi\right\rangle\geq 1-\delta

because
A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{b_{1},b_{2}}\geq 0
for any
a_{1},a_{2},b_{1},b_{2}
.
Then
Lemma

2.31
also implies that

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1}}\lVert(A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{i}})\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 2\delta.

Hence,
Lemma

2.35
implies that for all
i\in[2]
.

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{i}}\lVert\Big((G_{i})^{x}_{[g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{i}]}\otimes\mathds{1}-\mathds{1}\otimes(G_{i})^{x}_{[g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{i}]}\Big)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 18\delta.

Also, notice that

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{[g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{2}]}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},g_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle=\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1}}\sum_{g_{2},g_{2}^{\prime}}\left\langle\psi\right|(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}}(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}(G_{2})^{x}_{g_{2}^{\prime}}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\mathds{1}[g_{2}(y_{2})=g_{2}^{\prime}(y_{2})]\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1}}\sum_{a_{1}}\left\langle\psi\right|(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\otimes(G_{1})^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1})=a_{1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon.

Therefore,
\Delta_{3}=\Delta_{4}\leq 6\sqrt{2\delta}+\varepsilon
, and

\displaystyle\lvert S_{1}-S_{2}\rvert\leq\sum_{j=1}^{4}\Delta_{j}\leq 18\sqrt{2\delta}+2\varepsilon,

and

\displaystyle S_{1}\leq 32\delta+18\sqrt{2\delta}+2\varepsilon.

In conclusion,

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},y_{2}}\sum_{a_{1},a_{2}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes J^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{1},a_{2}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle-1\rvert

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2\delta}+\sqrt{32\delta+18\sqrt{2\delta}+2\varepsilon}\leq 11\delta^{1/4}+2\sqrt{\varepsilon},

and equivalently

\displaystyle A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{22\delta^{1/4}+4\sqrt{\epsilon}}\mathds{1}\otimes J^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{1},a_{2}]}.

Switching the roles of Alice and Bob, the same proof gives us that

\displaystyle J^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),g_{2}(y_{2})=a_{1},a_{2}]}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{22\delta^{1/4}+4\sqrt{\epsilon}}\mathds{1}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},y_{2}}_{a_{1},a_{2}}.

For the general case, assume

\displaystyle A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}}\otimes\mathds{1}\approx_{f(i,\delta,\varepsilon)}\mathds{1}\otimes J^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}\text{ and }

\displaystyle\mathds{1}\otimes A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}}\approx_{f(i,\delta,\varepsilon)}J^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}\otimes\mathds{1},

which imply that

\displaystyle\mathds{1}\otimes J^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})}\approx_{3(2\delta+2f(i,\delta,\varepsilon))}J^{x}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})}\otimes\mathds{1}.

Since
\delta
and
\varepsilon
are fixed, we write
f(i,\delta,\varepsilon)
as
f(i)
in the rest of the proof and proceed to the
i+1
case.
As in the base case, our goal is to bound

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}}\otimes J^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})}\otimes(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}\left|\psi\right\rangle.

by relating it to

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})}\otimes(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}\left|\psi\right\rangle

\displaystyle\approx_{\sqrt{2\delta}+\sqrt{f(i)}}\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}}\otimes\mathds{1}\left|\psi\right\rangle=1.

So the central step is bounding

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes\left(J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}-(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}\right)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}.

As in the base case, we can use similar arguments to show

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes

\displaystyle\Big(J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}(G_{i+1})^{x}_{[g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})=a_{i+1}]}

\displaystyle-(G_{i+1})^{x}_{[g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})=a_{i+1}]}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}\Big)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\leq 4(2f(i)+4\delta),

and

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes

\displaystyle\Big(J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}

\displaystyle-(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}\Big)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}-

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes

\displaystyle\Big(J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}(G_{i+1})^{x}_{[g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})=a_{i+1}]}

\displaystyle-(G_{i+1})^{x}_{[g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})=a_{i+1}]}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}\Big)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2f(i)+4\delta}+

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{6f(i)+4\delta}+2\varepsilon.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i},g_{i+1}}\lVert\mathds{1}\otimes\Big(J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}-(G_{i+1})^{x}_{g_{i+1}}J_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i}(y_{i})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i}]}^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i}}\Big)\left|\psi\right\rangle\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4(2f(i)+4\delta)+2\sqrt{2f(i)+4\delta}+2\sqrt{6f(i)+4\delta}+2\varepsilon,

and

\displaystyle\lvert\bigE_{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}\sum_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}}\left\langle\psi\right|A^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}}\otimes J^{x,y_{1},\ldots,y_{i+1}}_{[g_{1}(y_{1}),\ldots,g_{i+1}(y_{i+1})=a_{1},\ldots,a_{i+1}]}\left|\psi\right\rangle-1\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\delta}+\sqrt{f(i)}+\sqrt{16\sqrt{f(i)}+24\sqrt{\delta}+2\varepsilon}

That is
f(i+1)=5f(i)^{1/4}+7\delta^{1/4}+\sqrt{2\varepsilon}
. Then the lemma follows.
∎

Appendix C
Upper Bound on the Number of Noisy MES’s for Nonlocal Games

The proof follows closely to that of
[
QY21
]
. The major difference is that in the proof of
[
QY21
]
, each pair of questions
(x,y)
is treated independently. Then, a union bound is applied to all possible questions. To improve the upper bound, we take into account the distribution of the questions, combined with a better Gaussian dimension reduction in
[
QY23
]
.
Then
our new upper bound below only depends polynomially on the size of the question set
whereas the previous one has an exponential dependence.

C.1
Gaussian Dimension Reduction

The following lemma is a simplified version of
[
QY23
, Lemma 5.13]
, with the questions and answers being classical.
In the proof of
Theorem

5.5
,
we will use this lemma, after we replace the low-influence registers by Gaussian random variables, to further reduce the dimension of the Gaussian space.
The only difference is in Item 3 of
Lemma

C.1
, where we preserve the expectation of the
\zeta
function value over the random variable
\mathbf{M}
.
In the previous version (Item 2 of
[
QY23
, Lemma 5.13]
), we used Markov’s inequality on the expectation value.
As the notations are considerably different, we include a new proof for completeness.

Lemma C.1
.

[
QY23
, Lemma 5.13]

Given parameters
\rho\in\left[0,1\right]
,
\delta>0
,
d,n,h\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{>0}
,
m\geq 2
, an
m
-dimensional noisy MES
\psi_{AB}
with the quantum maximal correlation
\rho=\rho(\psi_{AB})
, and degree-d multilinear joint random matrices

\left(P(\mathbf{g}),Q(\mathbf{h})\right)=\left(\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\mathbf{g}_{S}P_{S},\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\mathbf{h}_{S}Q_{S}\right)_{\left(\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\right)\sim\mathcal{G}_{\rho}^{\otimes n}},

where
\mathbf{g}_{S}=\prod_{i\in S}\mathbf{g}_{i},\mathbf{h}_{S}=\prod_{i\in S}\mathbf{h}_{i}
and
P_{S},Q_{S}\in\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h}
for all
S\subseteq[n]
, satisfying

\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{g})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 1~\mbox{and}~\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{h})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 1.

Let
L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{n}\right)
be the space of random operators whose Fourier coefficients are square-integrable with respect to the measure
\gamma_{n}
. Then there exists an explicitly computable
n_{0}=n_{0}(d,\delta)
and maps
f_{M},g_{M}:L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{n}\right)\rightarrow L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{n}\right)
for
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n_{0}}
and joint random operators
\left(P(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}}),Q(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)=\left(f_{M}(P(\mathbf{g})),g_{M}(Q(\mathbf{h}))\right)
:

\left(P(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}}),Q(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)=\left(\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\mathbf{u}_{S}P_{S},\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\mathbf{v}_{S}Q_{S}\right)_{\left(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\right)\sim\mathcal{G}_{\rho}^{\otimes n_{0}}},

where
\tilde{\mathbf{x}}=\mathbf{x}/\lVert\mathbf{x}\rVert_{2}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{y}}=\mathbf{y}/\lVert\mathbf{y}\rVert_{2}
,
\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}=\prod_{i\in S}\left\langle m_{i},\tilde{\mathbf{x}}\right\rangle
,
\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{S}=\prod_{i\in S}\left\langle m_{i},\tilde{\mathbf{y}}\right\rangle
,
\left\langle\cdot,\cdot\right\rangle
denotes the standard inner product over
{\mathbb{R}}^{n_{0}}
and
m_{i}
denotes the
i
’th row of
M
, such that if we sample
\mathbf{M}\sim\gamma_{n\times n_{0}}
, then the following hold:

1.

With probability at least
1-2\delta
, we have

\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 1+\delta\quad\mbox{and}\quad\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 1+\delta.

2.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\otimes Q(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(\mathbf{g})\otimes Q(\mathbf{h})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]\right|\leq\delta.

3.

\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left(P(\mathbf{g})\right)\right]=\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left(P(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right)\right]\quad\mbox{and}\quad\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left(Q(\mathbf{h})\right)\right]=\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left(Q(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\right].

4.

the maps
f_{M},g_{M}
are linear and unital for any nonzero
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n_{0}}
.

In particular, one may take
n_{0}=\frac{d^{O(d)}}{\delta^{6}}
.

For
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n_{0}},
denote
F(M)=\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\otimes Q(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]
.
To prove
Lemma

C.1
item 2, we need the following lemma.

Lemma C.2
.

In the setting of
Lemma

C.1
, given
d\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{>0},

\delta>0,
there exists
n_{0}=\frac{d^{O(d)}}{\delta^{2}}
such that the following holds: For
\mathbf{M}\sim\gamma_{n\times n_{0}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(\mathbf{g})\otimes Q(\mathbf{h})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]\right|\leq~

\displaystyle\delta,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]\leq~

\displaystyle\delta.

We use the following lemma to prove
Lemma

C.2
.

Lemma C.3
.

[
GKR18
, Lemma A.8,A.9]

Given parameters
d
and
\delta
, there exists an explicitly computable
n_{0}(d,\delta)
such that the followings hold:

•

For any subsets
S,T\subseteq[n]
satisfying
\left|S\right|,\left|T\right|\leq d
, it holds that

\displaystyle\text{if }S\neq T:\quad\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\right]=0,

\displaystyle\text{if }S=T:\quad\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\right]-\rho^{\left|S\right|}\right|\leq\delta.

•

Let
(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\sim\mathcal{G}_{\rho}^{\otimes n_{0}}
be independent of
(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
, and let
\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}^{\prime}_{S}=\prod_{i\in S}\left\langle m_{i},\frac{\mathbf{x}^{\prime}}{\lVert\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\rVert_{2}}\right\rangle
,
\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}^{\prime}_{S}=\prod_{i\in S}\left\langle m_{i},\frac{\mathbf{y}^{\prime}}{\lVert\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\rVert_{2}}\right\rangle
. For any subsets
S,T,S^{\prime},T^{\prime}\subseteq[n]
satisfying
\left|S\right|,\left|T\right|,\left|S^{\prime}\right|,\left|T^{\prime}\right|\leq d
, it holds that

\text{if }S\bigtriangleup T\bigtriangleup S^{\prime}\bigtriangleup T^{\prime}\neq\emptyset:\\
\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x^{\prime}},\mathbf{y^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\mathbf{u^{\prime}}_{S^{\prime}}\mathbf{v^{\prime}}_{T^{\prime}}\right]-\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x^{\prime}},\mathbf{y^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{u^{\prime}}_{S^{\prime}}\mathbf{v^{\prime}}_{T^{\prime}}\right]\right)\right|=0,

\text{if }S\bigtriangleup T\bigtriangleup S^{\prime}\bigtriangleup T^{\prime}=\emptyset:\\
\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x^{\prime}},\mathbf{y^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\mathbf{u^{\prime}}_{S^{\prime}}\mathbf{v^{\prime}}_{T^{\prime}}\right]-\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x^{\prime}},\mathbf{y^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{u^{\prime}}_{S^{\prime}}\mathbf{v^{\prime}}_{T^{\prime}}\right]\right)\right|\leq\delta.

Here,
S\bigtriangleup T\bigtriangleup S^{\prime}\bigtriangleup T^{\prime}
is the symmetric difference of the sets
S,T,S^{\prime},T^{\prime}
, equivalently, the set of all
i\in[n]
which appear an odd number of times in the multiset
S\sqcup T\sqcup S^{\prime}\sqcup T^{\prime}
.

In particular, one may take
n_{0}=\frac{d^{O(d)}}{\delta^{2}}
.

Proof of
Lemma

C.2
.

Use
Lemma

C.3
with parameters
d
and
\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(\mathbf{g})\otimes Q(\mathbf{h})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{S,T\subseteq[n]}\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{g}_{S}\mathbf{h}_{T}\right]\right)\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P_{S}\otimes Q_{T}\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{S}\right]-\rho^{\left|S\right|}\right)\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P_{S}\otimes Q_{S}\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\delta\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P_{S}\otimes Q_{S}\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right|\quad\mbox{(\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:intermediate})}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\delta\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\quad\mbox{(\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{fac:cauchyschwartz})}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\delta\sqrt{\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\cdot\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\delta\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{g})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{h})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\quad\leq\delta.

Use
Lemma

C.3
with parameters
d
and
\delta\leftarrow\delta/9^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})^{2}\right]-\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\sum_{S,T,S^{\prime},T^{\prime}\subseteq[n]}\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{x^{\prime}},\mathbf{y^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\mathbf{u^{\prime}}_{S^{\prime}}\mathbf{v^{\prime}}_{T^{\prime}}\right]-\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{\mathbf{u}}_{S}\mathbf{\mathbf{v}}_{T}\right]\right)\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x^{\prime}},\mathbf{y^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathbf{u^{\prime}}_{S^{\prime}}\mathbf{v^{\prime}}_{T^{\prime}}\right]\right)\right|

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P_{S}\otimes Q_{S}\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P_{S^{\prime}}\otimes Q_{S^{\prime}}\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{9^{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}S,T,S^{\prime},T^{\prime}\subseteq[n]\\
S\bigtriangleup T\bigtriangleup S^{\prime}\bigtriangleup T^{\prime}=\emptyset\end{subarray}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S^{\prime}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T^{\prime}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}

To finish the proof, we will show that,

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}S,T,S^{\prime},T^{\prime}\subseteq[n]\\
S\bigtriangleup T\bigtriangleup S^{\prime}\bigtriangleup T^{\prime}=\emptyset\end{subarray}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S^{\prime}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T^{\prime}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\leq 9^{d}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{g})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{h})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]

Define functions
f,g:\left\{1,-1\right\}^{n}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}
over the boolean hypercube as,

f(x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}S\subseteq[n]\\
\left|S\right|\leq d\end{subarray}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\chi_{S}(x)\quad\text{and}\quad g(x)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}T\subseteq[n]\\
\left|T\right|\leq d\end{subarray}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\chi_{T}(x)

By the hypercontractivity inequality over the boolean hypercube
[
O’D13
, Page 240]

\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[f(x)^{4}\right]\leq 9^{d}\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[f(x)^{2}\right]\right)^{2}\quad\text{and}\quad\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[g(x)^{4}\right]\leq 9^{d}\left(\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[g(x)^{2}\right]\right)^{2},

we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}S,T,S^{\prime},T^{\prime}\subseteq[n]\\
S\bigtriangleup T\bigtriangleup S^{\prime}\bigtriangleup T^{\prime}=\emptyset\end{subarray}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S^{\prime}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{T^{\prime}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[f(x)^{2}g(x)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\sqrt{\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[f(x)^{4}\right]\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[g(x)^{4}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 9^{d}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[f(x)^{2}\right]\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}x\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[g(x)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 9^{d}\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\sum_{S\subseteq[n]}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q_{S}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 9^{d}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{g})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{h})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\quad\leq 9^{d}.

Thus
\mathrm{Var}\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]\leq\delta.

∎

To prove
Lemma

C.1
Item 1, we need the following lemma whose proof is similar to that of
Lemma

C.2
. We omit the proof here.

Lemma C.4
.

In the setting of
Lemma

C.1
, given
d\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{>0},

\delta>0,
there exists
n_{0}=\frac{d^{O(d)}}{\delta^{2}}
such that the following holds: For
\mathbf{M}\sim\gamma_{n\times n_{0}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{g})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\right|\leq~

\displaystyle\delta,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\right]\leq~

\displaystyle\delta,

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{h})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\right|\leq~

\displaystyle\delta,

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\right]\leq~

\displaystyle\delta.

Proof of
Lemma

C.1
.

For item 2, we invoke
Lemma

C.2
with parameters
d
and
\delta\leftarrow\delta^{3}/2
.
Using Chebyshev’s inequality, we have that for any
\eta>0
,

\Pr_{\mathbf{M}}\left[\left|F(\mathbf{M})-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]\right|>\eta\right]\leq\frac{\delta^{3}}{2\eta^{2}}.

Using the triangle inequality, we get

\displaystyle\Pr_{\mathbf{M}}\left[\left|F(\mathbf{M})-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(\mathbf{g})\otimes Q(\mathbf{h})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]\right|>\delta\right]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\Pr_{\mathbf{M}}\left[\left|F(\mathbf{M})-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]\right|+\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P(\mathbf{g})\otimes Q(\mathbf{h})\right)\psi_{AB}^{\otimes h}\right)\right]\right|>\delta\right]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\Pr_{\mathbf{M}}\left[\left|F(\mathbf{M})-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[F(\mathbf{M})\right]\right|>\delta-\delta^{3}/2\right]\leq\delta.

By
Lemma

C.4
, we can similarly argue for item 1. For item 3, note that for any fixed
x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n_{0}}
, the distribution of
\mathbf{M}x/\lVert x\rVert_{2}
is identical to
\gamma_{n}
.
It is easy to verify Item 4.
∎

C.2
Upper Bound

We are now ready to prove
Theorem

5.5
.

Proof of
Theorem

5.5
.

The proof follows that in
[
QY21
]
step by step, except that the Gaussian dimension reduction step in the original proof is replaced by
Lemma

C.1
.
Here, we include the proof for completeness.

Suppose the players use the strategy
\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}\right\}^{x\in\mathcal{X}}_{a\in\mathcal{A}},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}\right\}^{y\in\mathcal{Y}}_{b\in\mathcal{B}}\right)
to achieve the highest winning probability when sharing
n
copies of
\psi_{AB}
, where
{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}
is the POVM element of Alice corresponding to the answer
a
upon receiving the question
x
, and
{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}
is the POVM element of Bob corresponding to the answer
b
upon receiving the question
y
. Then for all
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
,
{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}\geq 0
,
{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}\geq 0
,
\sum_{a}{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}=\mathds{1}
,
\sum_{b}{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}=\mathds{1}
, and
\omega_{n}(\mathfrak{G},\psi_{AB})=\mathrm{val}_{n}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}\right\}\right)
.

Let
\delta,\tau
be parameters which are chosen later. The proof is composed of several steps.

•

Smoothing.
This step allows us to restrict ourselves to strategies with low-degree POVMs.

More specifically, for any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
, we apply the map
f^{(1)}
implied by
Lemma

A.1
to
{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}
and
{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}
to get
{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}
and
{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}
, respectively
10
10

10

Specifically, we apply a depolarizing channel
\Delta_{\gamma}
for some
\gamma\in(0,1)
to
{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}
and
{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}
, and then truncate it to be of degree
d
to get
{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}
. Readers may refer to
[
QY21
]
for details.
. Note that for all
x,y,a,b,

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\leq 1
and
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\leq 1.
Let
d=\frac{C\log^{2}\frac{1}{\delta}}{\delta(1-\rho)}
, by
Lemma

A.1
Item 3 and Item 4,

\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\otimes{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)-\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}\otimes{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|\leq\delta

and

\frac{1}{m^{n}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta({P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}})\leq\delta,\quad\frac{1}{m^{n}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta({Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}})\leq\delta.

By
Lemma

A.6
and
Lemma

A.1
items 1, 2 and 5, the following hold.

1.

For any
x,y,a,b
,
{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}
and
{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}
are of degree at most
d
.

2.

For any
x,y,a,b
,
\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\leq 1~\mbox{and}~{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\leq 1.

3.

\left|\mathrm{val}_{n}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right\}\right)-\mathrm{val}_{n}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(0)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(0)}_{b}}\right\}\right)\right|\leq\delta t^{2},

4.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m^{n}}\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right)\leq\delta t
and
\displaystyle\frac{1}{m^{n}}\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right)\leq\delta t.

5.

For any
x,y
,
\displaystyle\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}}{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}=\mathds{1}
.

•

Regularization.

In this step, we identify the set
H
of high-influence registers for all POVM elements.

For any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
, we apply
Lemma

A.3
to
{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}
and
{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}
to get sets
H_{x,a}
and
H_{y,b}
of size at most
d/\tau
, respectively, such that

\left(\forall i\notin H_{x,a}\right)~\mathrm{Inf}_{i}\left({P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right)\leq\tau\quad\mbox{and}\quad\left(\forall i\notin H_{y,b}\right)~\mathrm{Inf}_{i}\left({Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right)\leq\tau.

Set
H=\left(\bigcup_{x,a}H_{x,a}\right)\cup\left(\bigcup_{y,b}H_{y,b}\right)
, then

h=\left|H\right|\leq\frac{2std}{\tau}
, and

\left(\forall i\notin H\right)~\mathrm{Inf}_{i}\left({P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right)\leq\tau\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mathrm{Inf}_{i}\left({Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right)\leq\tau.

•

Invariance from
\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes n}
to
L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{(m^{2}-1)(n-h)}\right)
.

In this step, we only keep the quantum registers in
H
and replace
the rest of the quantum registers by Gaussian random variables.
Hence,
the number of quantum registers is reduced from
n
to
h=\lvert H\rvert=d/\tau
.

For any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
, applying
[
QY21
, Lemma 10.5]
to
{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}
,
{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}
and
H
, we obtain joint random matrices

\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g}),{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})\right)\in L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{2(m^{2}-1)(n-h)}\right)\times L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{2(m^{2}-1)(n-h)}\right),

where
\left(\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\right)\sim\mathcal{G}_{\rho}^{\otimes 2(m^{2}-1)(n-h)}
, such that the following hold.

1.

For any
x,y,a,b,

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 1
and
\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 1
.

2.

\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{g})\right\}\right)\right]=\mathrm{val}_{n}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right\}\right).

3.

\displaystyle\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\left|\frac{1}{m^{h}}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right)\right]-\frac{1}{m^{n}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(1)}_{a}}\right)\right|\leq O\left(t\left(3^{d}m^{d/2}\sqrt{\tau}d\right)^{2/3}\right)
and

\displaystyle\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\left|\frac{1}{m^{h}}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})}\right)\right]-\frac{1}{m^{n}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(1)}_{b}}\right)\right|\leq O\left(t\left(3^{d}m^{d/2}\sqrt{\tau}d\right)^{2/3}\right)
.

4.

For any
x,y
,
\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}}{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})=\mathds{1}.

•

Gaussian dimension reduction.

In this step, we apply
Lemma

C.1
to further reduce the number of Gaussian random variables.
This is the only part different from the proof in
[
QY21
]
.

Let
n_{0}
be determined later.
For any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
and
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times n_{0}}
, applying
Lemma

C.1
to
{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})
and
{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})
with
\delta\leftarrow\delta/\left(2s^{2}t^{2}\right)
,
d\leftarrow d
,
n\leftarrow 2(m^{2}-1)(n-h)
, we get joint random matrices
{P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})
and
{Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})
. If we sample
\mathbf{M}\sim\gamma_{n\times n_{0}}
, by
Lemma

C.1
item 3 we have

\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right)\right]=\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right)\right]

and

\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{M},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\right]=\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})\right)\right].

Then by Markov’s inequality, with probability each at most
1/6
,

\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right)\right]>6\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right)\right]

and

\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(\mathbf{M}\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\right]>6\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})\right)\right].

By
Lemma

C.1
item 1, 2, and using a union bound, with probability at least
2/3-\delta
the following hold:

1.

For any
x,y,a,b
,
\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2
and

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2.

2.

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right\}\right)\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g},\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{g})\right\}\right)\right]\right|\leq\delta t^{2}.

3.

\displaystyle\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right)\right]\leq 6\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{g}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}(\mathbf{g})\right)\right]
and

\displaystyle\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\right]\leq 6\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{h}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}(\mathbf{h})\right)\right].

4.

For any
x,y
,
\displaystyle\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}{P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}}{Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})=\mathds{1}.

Here
n_{0}=\frac{d^{O(d)}s^{12}t^{12}}{\delta^{6}}
. Therefore, there must exist an
M
such that all the above four requirements hold. We will use this fixed M throughout the rest of the proof.

Smoothing random matrices.

In this step, we reduce
\deg({P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}})
and
\deg({Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}})
for any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
. We apply
[
QY21
, Lemma 12.1]
to
{P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})
and
{Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})
with
\delta\leftarrow\delta
,

,
n\leftarrow n_{0}
and obtain joint random matrices
{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x}),{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\in L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{n_{0}}\right)
such that the following holds.

1.

For any
x,y,a,b
, the entries of
{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})
and
{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})
are polynomials of degree at most
d
.

2.

For any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2
and

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2.

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{x})\right\}\right)\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right\}\right)\right]\right|\leq\delta t^{2}.

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right]-\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(3)}_{a}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{x}})\right)\right]\right|\leq\delta t
and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\right)\right]-\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(3)}_{b}}(M\tilde{\mathbf{y}})\right)\right]\right|\leq\delta t.

5.

For any
x,y
,
\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}}{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})=\mathds{1}.

Multilinearization.

For any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}

[
QY21
, Lemma 13.1]
to
{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})
and
{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})
with
d\leftarrow d
,
\delta\leftarrow\tau
,
h\leftarrow h
,
n\leftarrow n_{0}
and obtain joint random matrices
{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x}),{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\in L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{n_{0}n_{1}}\right)
such that the following holds.

1.

x,y,a,b
, the entries of
{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})
and
{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})
are multilinear polynomials of degree at most
d

and

\tau

For any
x,y,a,b

\displaystyle\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right]\leq 2

\displaystyle\left|\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{x})\right\}\right)\right]-\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\right\}\right)\right]\right|\leq\tau t^{2}.

4.

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right]-\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(4)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right]\right|\leq\tau t
and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\right)\right]-\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(4)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\right)\right]\right|\leq\tau t.

For any
x,y
,
\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}}{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})=\mathds{1}.

n_{1}=O\left(\frac{d^{2}}{\tau^{2}}\right).

Invariance from
L^{2}\left(\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h},\gamma_{n_{0}n_{1}}\right)
to
\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h+n_{0}n_{1}}
.

In this step, we transform all the random matrices from the previous step to matrices without any classical randomness.
In particular, we replace all the Gaussian random variables with
n_{0}n_{1}
quantum registers, so after this step, the number of quantum registers is
h+n_{0}n_{1}
.

For any
\left(x,y,a,b\right)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}
, applying
[
QY21
, Lemma 10.11]
to
{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})
,
{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})
with
n\leftarrow n_{0}n_{1}
,
h\leftarrow h
,
d\leftarrow 2d
,
\tau\leftarrow\tau
to get
{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}},{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\in\mathcal{H}_{m}^{\otimes h+n_{0}n_{1}}
satisfying the following.

1.

For any
x,y,a,b
,
\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\leq 2
and
\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\leq 2.

2.

\mathrm{val}_{h+n_{0}n_{1}}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right\}\right)=\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{val}_{h}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})\right\}\right)\right].

3.

\displaystyle\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\left|\frac{1}{m^{h+n_{0}n_{1}}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)-\frac{1}{m^{h}}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(5)}_{a}}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right]\right|\leq O\left(t\left(9^{d}m^{d}\sqrt{\tau}d\right)^{2/3}\right)
and

\displaystyle\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\left|\frac{1}{m^{h+n_{0}n_{1}}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right)-\frac{1}{m^{h}}\bigE_{\begin{subarray}{c}\end{subarray}}\>\!\!\left[\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({{Q^{\,y,(5)}_{b}}(\mathbf{y})}\right)\right]\right|\leq O\left(t\left(9^{d}m^{d}\sqrt{\tau}d\right)^{2/3}\right)
.

4.

For any
x,y
,
\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}=\sum_{b\in\mathcal{B}}{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}=\mathds{1}.

•

Rounding.
Note that the matrices from the previous step may not form valid POVMs, so in this step we round them to close POVMs. In this step, the number of quantum registers remains the same as
h+n_{0}n_{1}
.

By
Lemma

A.4
there exist operators
\left\{{P^{\,x,(7)}_{a}}\right\}
and
\left\{{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}\right\}
satisfying for all
x

\displaystyle\sum_{a}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(7)}_{a}}-{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3(t+1)}{m^{D}}\sum_{a}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)+6\sqrt{t}\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{a}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq 10t\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{a}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)\right)^{1/2}.

(36)

Similarly, for all
y
, we have

\sum_{a}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}-{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\leq 10t\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{b}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right)\right)^{1/2}.

(37)

Then

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{val}_{D}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(7)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}\right\}\right)-\mathrm{val}_{D}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right\}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{val}_{D}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(7)}_{a}}-{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}\right\}\right)\right|+\left|\mathrm{val}_{D}\left(\left\{{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}-{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right\}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\sum_{x,y,a,b}\mu(x,y)\left({\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(7)}_{a}}-{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}-{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{b}\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x){\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(7)}_{a}}-{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{a}\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y){\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{b}\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x){\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{a}\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y){\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}-{Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\quad\mbox{(Cauchy Schwarz)}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\sqrt{10}t^{2}\left(\sum_{x}\mu_{A}(x)\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{a}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)\right)^{1/2}\right)^{1/2}+2\sqrt{5}t^{2}\left(\sum_{y}\mu_{B}(y)\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{b}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right)\right)^{1/2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\sqrt{10}t^{2}\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)\right)^{1/4}+2\sqrt{5}t^{2}\left(\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right)\right)^{1/4},

where in the second last inequality, we use
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}\leq 2
,
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(7)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}\leq 1
, and
Eqs.

36
and
37
.
The last inequality follows from concavity of the function
x\mapsto\sqrt{x}
.

Keeping track of the parameters in the construction, we can upper bound
\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{x,a}\mu_{A}(x)\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(6)}_{a}}\right)
and
\frac{1}{m^{D}}\sum_{y,b}\mu_{B}(y)\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(6)}_{b}}\right)
.
We choose

\delta=\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{300t^{9}},\tau=\frac{\epsilon^{12}}{t^{27}}\exp\left(-\frac{300t^{9}\log m}{\epsilon^{4}(1-\rho)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{t}{\epsilon}\right)\right)

(38)

such that the difference in the game value at the final step matches that of the previous steps, remaining on the order of
O(\delta t^{2})
. We conclude that the number of quantum registers is

\displaystyle D=h+n_{0}n_{1}=\frac{d}{\tau}+\frac{d^{O(d)}s^{12}t^{12}}{\delta^{6}}\cdot O\left(\frac{d^{2}}{\tau^{2}}\right)=O\left(\frac{s^{12}t^{120}}{\epsilon^{48}}\exp\left(\frac{600t^{9}\log m}{\epsilon^{4}(1-\rho)}\log^{2}\left(\frac{t}{\epsilon(1-\rho)}\right)\right)\right),

which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Truncation

Lemma D.1

(Truncation)
.

Let
\left\{P^{\,x}_{a}\right\},\left\{Q^{\,y}_{b}\right\}
be two sets of operators satisfying

1.

For all
x,y
,
\sum_{a}P^{\,x}_{a}=\sum_{b}Q^{\,y}_{b}=\mathds{1}
.

2.

For all
x,a,y,b,\sigma
,
\left|\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)\right|\leq 1
and
\left|\widehat{Q}^{\,y}_{b}(\sigma)\right|\leq 1
.

Let
{s_{w}}=D\log m+\log\left(\frac{2}{\delta}\right)
.
Then there exist operators
\left\{{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\right\},\left\{{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right\}
satisfying

1.

For each
x,y,a,b,\sigma
, the Fourier coefficients of
{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}
and
{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}
consists of at most
{s_{w}}
bits.

2.

For all
x,y
,
\sum_{a}{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}=\sum_{b}{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}=\mathds{1}
.

3.

For all
x,y,a,b
,
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\leq 1
and
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\leq 1
.

4.

For all
x,y,a,b
,
\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\otimes{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)-\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P^{\,x}_{a}\otimes Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|\leq\delta
.

5.

For all
x,y,a,b
,

\left|\frac{1}{m^{D}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\right)-\frac{1}{m^{D}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left(P^{\,x}_{a}\right)\right|\leq\delta~\mbox{and}~\left|\frac{1}{m^{D}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left({Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right)-\frac{1}{m^{D}}\mathrm{Tr}~\zeta\left(Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\right|\leq\delta.

Proof.

Let
\alpha=2^{-{s_{w}}}=\delta/(2m^{D})
.
For each
x,y,\sigma
, define
{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)=\lfloor\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)/\alpha\rfloor\alpha
.
For each
x,\sigma\neq 0^{D}
, define integer
k_{x,\sigma}
as

-\sum_{a}{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)=k_{x,\sigma}\cdot\alpha

and for
\sigma=0^{D}
, define

1-\sum_{a}{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)=k_{x,0^{D}}\cdot\alpha.

Let
t_{x,\sigma}=\left|\left\{a\in\mathcal{A}:{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)\neq\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)\right\}\right|
,
we can see that
0\leq k_{x,\sigma}<t_{x,\sigma}
always holds because

\sum_{a}P^{\,x}_{a}=\mathds{1}

and by the fact that
{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)>\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)-\alpha
.
Let
S_{x,\sigma}
be an arbitrary subset of
\left\{a\in\mathcal{A}:{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)\neq\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)\right\}
of size
k_{x,\sigma}
.
Define
{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}
as

{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(2)}}_{a}}(\sigma)=\begin{cases}{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)&\text{ if }a\not\in S_{x,\sigma}\\
{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)+\alpha&\text{ if }a\in S_{x,\sigma}\\
\end{cases}

Then item 1 and item 2 hold for
{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}
.
Also, since for
a\in S_{x,\sigma}
we have
{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)<\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)\leq 1
, we have
{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(1)}}_{a}}(\sigma)\leq 1-\alpha
.
So, it can be verified that
\left|{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(2)}}_{a}}(\sigma)\right|\leq 1
always holds, which implies that item 3 also holds.
To prove the remaining items, we need

{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|P^{\,x}_{a}-{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}=\sqrt{\sum_{\sigma}\left(\widehat{P}^{\,x}_{a}(\sigma)-{{\widehat{P}}^{\,x,{(2)}}_{a}}(\sigma)\right)^{2}}<\sqrt{\sum_{\sigma}\alpha^{2}}\leq m^{D}\alpha.

We can apply the same operations to
\left\{Q^{\,y}_{b}\right\}
and get
\left\{{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right\}
.
Then for all
x,y,a,b
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\otimes{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)-\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P^{\,x}_{a}\otimes Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\otimes{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)-\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\otimes Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\otimes Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)-\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(P^{\,x}_{a}\otimes Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\otimes\left({Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}-Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|+\left|\mathrm{Tr}\left(\left(\left({P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}-P^{\,x}_{a}\right)\otimes Q^{\,y}_{b}\right)\psi^{\otimes n}_{AB}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{Q^{\,y,(2)}_{b}}-Q^{\,y}_{b}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|{P^{\,x,(2)}_{a}}-P^{\,x}_{a}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|Q^{\,y}_{b}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}\leq 2m^{D}\alpha=\delta,

and item 4 follows.
Then item 5 follows from
2.20
.
∎
